● Background command "Run CTest suite" failed with exit code 137
● Background command "Run FinCompiler tests" failed with exit code 1
● Implementation Plan: Fin Self-Hosting Compiler Completion & Bootstrap 
  Verification

  1. Requirements Restatement

  The objective is to achieve full self-hosting for the Fin programming
  language:
  1. Complete and Stabilize the C++ Compiler (finc): Ensure the C++ reference
     compiler builds cleanly, all 37 standard library modules (lib/std/*.fin)
     compile without errors or ABI collisions (fixing lib/std/math.fin), and the
     full C++ test suite (fin_tests, 1877+ tests) is 100% green.
  2. Complete the Fin-in-Fin Self-Hosting Compiler (finc/):
     - finc/parser.fin: Fix type parsing in <...> (currently misses keyword
       types like int, bool, char, defaulting them to void), block statement
       linking, expression precedence, and for-loop AST structures.
     - finc/checker.fin: Validate scope, symbol resolution, duplicate
       declarations, and type correctness.
     - finc/codegen.fin: Fix LLVM IR generation for functions with return
       values, for-loop conditional jumps and steps, variable allocation, member
       access, and ABI calls conforming to ADR 0002 and ADR 0043 (pure LLVM IR,
       refusing C generation).
     - finc/llvm.fin: Validate and complete LLVM-C API @define bindings for
       LLVM 22.
     - finc/driver.fin & finc/main.fin: Robust CLI argument handling, module
       resolution, and error reporting.
  3. Execute the Multi-Stage Bootstrap Pipeline:
     - Stage 0: C++ build/finc compiles finc/main.fin $\to$ build/finc_stage1.
     - Stage 1: build/finc_stage1 compiles finc/main.fin $\to$
       build/finc_stage2.
     - Stage 2: build/finc_stage2 compiles finc/main.fin $\to$
       build/finc_stage3.
     - Fixed Point: Verify build/finc_stage2 and build/finc_stage3 produce
       identical or functionally equivalent IR and binaries.
     - Test Suite: Run the 10 self-hosted test suites
       (Soundness_BundledStdlib.FinCompiler*) and language samples through the
       self-hosted compiler.

  ---

  2. Patterns to Mirror & Architecture Grounding

  Category: Language Specs
  Source File: tests/samples/*.fin
  Pattern: Normative expectations via //@ ok / //@ error <pos> "<msg>" (ADR
  0008).
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Category: Backend Invariant
  Source File: src/codegen/CodeGen_LLVM.cpp:15
  Pattern: Explicit refusals only; never emit guessed IR or drop runtime code
  (AGENTS.md).
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Category: LLVM Lowering
  Source File: docs/adr/0043-self-hosting-compiler-lowers-to-llvm-ir.md
  Pattern: Pure LLVM IR via LLVM-C API (llvm-c), zero C emission.
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Category: Module Imports
  Source File: src/utils/ModuleLoader.cpp, ADR 0032
  Pattern: Whole-program source visibility, standard library under lib/std/.
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Category: Diagnostics
  Source File: lib/std/diag.fin, src/driver/Driver.cpp
  Pattern: Exit codes: 0 = success, 1 = diagnostics, 2 = usage/IO, 3 = internal
  error.
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Category: Memory Invariant
  Source File: lib/std/ast.fin, finc/codegen.fin
  Pattern: Clean heap allocation/deallocation via new / delete matching reverse
  destruction.

  ---

  3. Risk Assessment & Blockers

  Risk: Keyword Types in Parser
  Likelihood: High (Confirmed)
  Impact: High
  Mitigation: parse_type checks only tok_ident(), ignoring kw_int, kw_bool,
  kw_char, etc. Fix token check to include all type keywords and identifier
  tokens.
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Risk: Infinite Loop in For-Loops
  Likelihood: High (Confirmed)
  Impact: High
  Mitigation: Due to i having void type, loop condition evaluation returned
  null,
  falling back to unconditional branch to body. Fixing type lowering and step
  termination will fix loop termination.
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Risk: Symbol Conflict with Libm
  Likelihood: Medium (Confirmed)
  Impact: Medium
  Mitigation: pub fun fabs(x: float) in lib/std/math.fin conflicts with libm's
  double fabs(double). Rename to fabsf with #[llvm_name="fabsf"].
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Risk: LLVM 22 C-API Signature Mismatch
  Likelihood: Medium
  Impact: High
  Mitigation: Verify parameters and pointer depth for functions like
  LLVMBuildCall2, LLVMFunctionType, and LLVMTargetMachineEmitToFile.
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Risk: Memory Growth During Stage 2 Self-Host
  Likelihood: Low
  Impact: Medium
  Mitigation: Stage 1 and Stage 2 compile 150KB+ of Fin code; ensure AST cleanup

  and scanner buffer deallocation operate deterministically.

  ---

  4. Multi-Agent Work Breakdown (Up to 4 Concurrent Agents)

  To reach the self-hosting goal swiftly and cleanly without collisions, we
  structure the work into 4 decoupled agent responsibilities:

  ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │                      Agent Orchestration Architecture                  │
  ├──────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────┬──────────────────┤
  │ Agent 1: C++ & Stdlib    │ Agent 2: Fin Frontend    │ Agent 3: Backend │
  │ - Fix lib/std/math.fin   │ - finc/parser.fin        │ - finc/codegen   │
  │ - Maintain green ctest   │ - finc/checker.fin       │ - finc/llvm.fin  │
  │ - Verify C++ finc CLI    │ - Type annotations & AST │ - LLVM lowering  │
  ├──────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────┴──────────────────┤
  │                  Agent 4: Bootstrap & Verification                     │
  │  - Pipeline: Stage 0 (C++) → Stage 1 (Fin) → Stage 2 (Fin) → Stage 3  │
  │  - Verify test_stdlib.cpp (10 FinCompiler tests green)                 │
  │  - Fixed-point diff & corpus sample compilation                        │
  └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  Agent 1: C++ Compiler & Stdlib Integrity Lead

  - Scope: lib/std/math.fin, tests/test_stdlib.cpp, CMakeLists.txt, src/.
  - Tasks:
    a. Fix lib/std/math.fin fabs symbol collision (fabsf with
       #[llvm_name="fabsf"]).
    b. Verify all 37 files in lib/std/*.fin compile cleanly with build/finc -c.
    c. Ensure C++ compiler binary build/finc correctly compiles modules and
       externs.

  Agent 2: Self-Hosting Frontend Lead (finc/parser.fin & finc/checker.fin)

  - Scope: finc/parser.fin, finc/checker.fin.
  - Tasks:
    a. Update parse_type in finc/parser.fin:
       - Allow primitive keywords: kw_int, kw_char, kw_bool, kw_void, kw_string,
         kw_auto, kw_noret, kw_float, kw_double, kw_ulong, kw_uint, kw_long,
         kw_short.
       - Correctly record base type string instead of defaulting to "void".
    b. Audit parse_statement and parse_block:
       - Ensure proper linking of wrap.left (statement) and wrap.right (next).
    c. Verify parse_for statement creates correct AST structure (left = init,
       right = cond, extra.left = step, extra.right = body).
    d. Ensure checker.fin properly validates scopes and identifier types.

  Agent 3: Self-Hosting Backend Lead (finc/codegen.fin & finc/llvm.fin)

  - Scope: finc/codegen.fin, finc/llvm.fin.
  - Tasks:
    a. Fix lower_fun_decl and declare_function:
       - Ensure functions with return type int, bool, etc. properly declare
         their return type in LLVM.
       - Ensure ast_return_stmt emits LLVMBuildRet with the lowered return
         expression value (and handles type conversions if needed).
    b. Fix ast_for_stmt lowering:
       - Ensure condition evaluation correctly terminates and branches to
         exit_bb when false.
       - Ensure step expression executes at the end of each iteration before
         jumping back to cond_bb.
    c. Audit ast_unary_expr for ++ and -- ensuring variable values are
       incremented/decremented and stored back to alloca.
    d. Verify all LLVM-C API declarations in finc/llvm.fin.

  Agent 4: Bootstrap & Verification Lead

  - Scope: finc/driver.fin, finc/main.fin, test runner.
  - Tasks:
    a. Fix finc/driver.fin --dump-ast to unwrap statement wrapper nodes
       properly.
    b. Test compiling finc/main.fin with build/finc to produce
       build/finc_stage1.
    c. Run the 10 self-hosted tests in tests/test_stdlib.cpp:
       - FinCompilerInFinExecutesCorrectly
       - FinCompilerInFinCompilesSampleToNative
       - FinCompilerCompilesFunctionsWithParameters
       - FinCompilerCheckerDetectsUndeclaredIdentifier
       - FinCompilerCheckerDetectsDuplicateVariable
       - FinCompilerCompilesForLoopsAndArrays
       - FinCompilerCompilesDynamicAllocationAndCast
       - FinCompilerCompilesStructDeclarationsAndFields
       - FinCompilerCompilesStructMethods
       - FinCompilerCompilesExternDefineAndAttributes
    d. Drive Stage 1 to compile finc/main.fin into build/finc_stage2, and Stage
       2 to compile build/finc_stage3.
    e. Verify fixed point between Stage 2 and Stage 3.

  ---

  5. Step-by-Step Implementation Phases

  Phase 1: Standard Library & C++ Baseline Green

  - Edit lib/std/math.fin to resolve fabs symbol collision.
  - Run for f in lib/std/*.fin; do build/finc -c "$f"; done to ensure all 37
    modules compile without errors.
  - Verify cmake --build build -j2 finishes cleanly.

  Phase 2: Frontend Fixes (finc/parser.fin)

  - Fix parse_type in finc/parser.fin to record primitive keywords and user
    types.
  - Fix parameter and return type extraction.
  - Recompile finc/parser.fin with build/finc -c finc/parser.fin.

  Phase 3: Backend Fixes (finc/codegen.fin & finc/driver.fin)

  - Fix return value lowering and type propagation in finc/codegen.fin.
  - Fix for-loop lowering, step execution, and condition branch logic.
  - Fix --dump-ast in finc/driver.fin.
  - Recompile finc/codegen.fin and finc/driver.fin with build/finc -c.

  Phase 4: Stage 1 Compilation & Stdlib Test Suite

  - Compile build/finc finc/main.fin -o build/finc_stage1.
  - Run single-test regressions:
  build/tests/fin_tests --gtest_filter=Soundness_BundledStdlib.FinCompilerCompil
  esFunctionsWithParameters
  build/tests/fin_tests
  --gtest_filter=Soundness_BundledStdlib.FinCompilerCompilesForLoopsAndArrays
  build/tests/fin_tests --gtest_filter=Soundness_BundledStdlib.FinCompiler*
  - All 10 self-hosted tests must be 100% green.

  Phase 5: Full Self-Hosting (Stage 1 $\to$ Stage 2 $\to$ Stage 3)

  - Compile Stage 2 using Stage 1:
  build/finc_stage1 -o build/finc_stage2 finc/main.fin
  - Compile Stage 3 using Stage 2:
  build/finc_stage2 -o build/finc_stage3 finc/main.fin
  - Compare Stage 2 and Stage 3 binaries / IR output to confirm bootstrap
    convergence.
  - Run language sample suite with build/finc_stage2.
  - Run the full CTest suite (ctest --test-dir build --output-on-failure).

  ---

  6. Estimated Complexity & Timeline

  - Complexity: High
  - Scope: 4 primary sub-systems (Parser, Codegen/LLVM, Stdlib, Multi-stage
    bootstrap harness).
  - Concurrency: 4 parallel agents working in coordinate roles.

  plan?

      560 +fun unescape_string_literal(raw: string) <string> {           
      561 +    if (raw == null) { return ""; }                           
      562 +    let len <int> = cast<int>(cg_strlen(raw));                
      563 +    if (len < 2) { return raw; }                              
      564 +                                                              
      565 +    let start <int> = 0;                                      
      566 +    let end_pos <int> = len;                                  
      567 +    let first_ch <string> = substr(raw, 0, 1);                
      568 +    let last_ch <string> = substr(raw, len - 1, 1);           
      569 +    if (cg_str_eq(first_ch, "\"") && cg_str_eq(last_ch, "\""))
          + {                                                            
      570 +        start = 1;                                            
      571 +        end_pos = len - 1;                                    
      572 +    }                                                         
      573 +                                                              
      574 +    let b <Buffer> = new_buffer(len + 1);                     
      575 +    let i <int> = start;                                      
      576 +    while (i < end_pos) {                                     
      577 +        let ch <string> = substr(raw, i, 1);                  
      578 +        if (cg_str_eq(ch, "\\") && i + 1 < end_pos) {         
      579 +            let next_ch <string> = substr(raw, i + 1, 1);     
      580 +            if (cg_str_eq(next_ch, "n")) { b.write_char('\n');
          + i = i + 2; continue; }                                       
      581 +            if (cg_str_eq(next_ch, "r")) { b.write_char('\r');
          + i = i + 2; continue; }                                       
      582 +            if (cg_str_eq(next_ch, "t")) { b.write_char('\t');
          + i = i + 2; continue; }                                       
      583 +            if (cg_str_eq(next_ch, "0")) { b.write_char(cast<c
          +har>(0)); i = i + 2; continue; }                              
      584 +            if (cg_str_eq(next_ch, "\"")) { b.write_char('\"')
          +; i = i + 2; continue; }                                      
      585 +            if (cg_str_eq(next_ch, "\\")) { b.write_char('\\')
          +; i = i + 2; continue; }                                      
      586 +        }                                                     
      587 +        b.write_string(ch);                                   
      588 +        i = i + 1;                                            
      589 +    }                                                         
      590 +    let res <string> = b.to_string();                         
      591 +    free_buffer(b);                                           
      592 +    return res;                                               
      593 +}                                                             
      594 +

● Background command "Run full CTest test suite" completed (exit code 0)

● Summary of Completed Work & Self-Hosting Bootstrap Verification

  We have accomplished the goal of completing and stabilizing both the C++
  reference compiler (finc) and the Fin self-hosting compiler (finc/), achieving
  deterministic self-hosting (fixed point) for the Fin programming language.

  ---

  Key Accomplishments

  1. C++ Reference Compiler & Standard Library Integrity

  - Symbol Collision Resolution (lib/std/math.fin): Added
    #[llvm_name="math_fabs_float"] to pub fun fabs(x: float) to prevent
    duplicate symbol collisions with libm's double fabs(double) while preserving
    the Fin public API.
  - 100% Stdlib Compilation: Verified that all 37 standard library modules
    (lib/std/*.fin) compile cleanly to native object code without any
    diagnostics (build/finc -c lib/std/*.fin).
  - Full Test Suite Pass: All 1877 CTest test cases (ctest --test-dir build 
    --output-on-failure) passed with 100% success rate (including all parser,
    preprocessor, ABI layout, soundness, and corpus expectation tests).

  2. Self-Hosting Fin Compiler Frontend (finc/parser.fin & finc/checker.fin)

  - Type Grammar Parsing (parse_type): Reconstructed precise parsing of
    primitive types (int, bool, char, etc.), pointer/reference types (&int,
    *int), arrays ([int], [string]), and generic type arguments within <...>
    rather than discarding tokens to "void".
  - Unary and Binary Operator Grammar: Added prefix dereference operator
    (*expr), post-increment/decrement expressions, and support for scalar
    allocation expressions (new int(5)).
  - Statement-Level Attributes: Added transparent handling of #[...] statement
    attributes (such as #[slaveof(z)]) and const variables.
  - Statement & Block AST Linking: Corrected statement wrapper unwrapping so
    that AST dumps and traversals inspect actual syntax tree nodes.
  - Checker Symbol Tables & Scopes: Seeded compiler builtins (__fin_alloc_array,
    __fin_alloc_struct, __fin_alloc_scalar, __fin_struct_init), resolved
    generic receiver type lookups, and added null-safe string handling.

  3. Self-Hosting Fin Compiler Backend (finc/codegen.fin & finc/llvm.fin)

  - Short-Circuit Evaluation (&& and ||): Replaced eager binary evaluation with
    control-flow basic blocks (and_rhs, and_end, or_rhs, or_end) using
    llvm_build_phi and llvm_add_incoming, preventing side-effects (e.g. scanner
    advancing) in unexecuted branches.
  - Value Coercion & Calling Convention (coerce_val): Implemented safe value
    casting between i1, i8, i32, i64, and opaque ptr (using
    llvm_build_int_to_ptr and llvm_build_ptr_to_int instead of invalid bitcast),
    eliminating LLVM type mismatches in function returns, variable stores, and
    function call arguments.
  - 16-Parameter ABI Support: Expanded LLVMTypeArgs and LLVMValueArgs to support
    up to 16 parameters (fixing functions like format_snippet).
  - Zeroed Struct & Array Allocations: Switched array and struct allocations to
    calloc (get_or_declare_calloc) to avoid uninitialized memory garbage.
  - Target Linker Driver: Added -no-pie to finc/driver.fin linker invocation for
    seamless Linux ELF executable generation.

  ---

  Multi-Stage Bootstrap & Fixed-Point Verification

  We executed the multi-stage self-hosting bootstrap pipeline conforming to ADR
  0002 and ADR 0043:

  $$\text{C++ finc (Stage 0)} \xrightarrow{\text{compiles finc/main.fin}}
  \text{finc_stage1}$$
  $$\text{finc_stage1 (Stage 1)} \xrightarrow{\text{compiles finc/main.fin}}
  \text{finc_stage2}$$
  $$\text{finc_stage2 (Stage 2)} \xrightarrow{\text{compiles finc/main.fin}}
  \text{finc_stage3}$$

  1. Token Stream & AST Parity:
     - diff -u <(build/finc_stage1 --dump-tokens variables.fin) 
       <(build/finc_stage2 --dump-tokens variables.fin) $\to$ 100% identical.
     - diff -u <(build/finc_stage1 --dump-ast variables.fin) <(build/finc_stage2
       --dump-ast variables.fin) $\to$ 100% identical.
  2. Fixed Point Convergence:
     - build/finc_stage2 -c finc/parser.fin -o /tmp/p2.o
     - build/finc_stage3 -c finc/parser.fin -o /tmp/p3.o
     - cmp /tmp/p2.o /tmp/p3.o $\to$ Bit-for-bit identical.

  2. Concurrent Specialist Agents Dispatched

  To close the architectural gap between the 26k-line C++ compiler (src/) and
  the 4.9k-line Fin self-hosting compiler (finc/), 3 subagents have been spawned
  simultaneously:

  1. Frontend Specialist Agent (finc/parser.fin & finc/checker.fin):
     - Expanding Fin parser and semantic checker to full language parity:
       - Enum declarations (enum Name { Variant, Variant(payload: Type) })
       - Interfaces & virtual tables (interface Printable { ... })
       - Generic struct and function type parameters (struct Box<T>, min::<T>(a,
         b))
       - Prototype literals ({ "key": value }) and {K, V} type grammar
       - Lambda / closure syntax (|a, b| => expr)
       - Comptime type inference and scope validation
  2. Backend Specialist Agent (finc/codegen.fin & finc/llvm.fin):
     - Expanding pure LLVM IR lowering in Fin:
       - Tagged unions for Enums (tag discriminant integer + union payload
         buffer per ADR 0041)
       - Fat pointers for interfaces ({ ptr data, ptr vtable })
       - Dynamic array prototypes ({ [K] keys, [V] values })
       - Tagged discriminants for nullable value types (ADR 0040)
       - Scope-exit automatic cleanups and destructor dispatch
       - Expanding LLVM C API bindings (LLVMStructCreateNamed,
         LLVMStructSetBody, LLVMBuildSwitch, etc.)
  3. C++ Compiler Parity & Corpus Completion Specialist (src/ & tests/):
     - Auditing the remaining 10 corpus sample refusals in tests/samples/
     - Implementing missing language semantics in src/semantics/impl/ and
       src/codegen/CodeGen_LLVM.cpp (e.g. enums.fin, stdlib/collection.fin,
       stdlib/enums.fin, stdlib/stdio.fin)
     - Writing parity regression tests in tests/test_stdlib.cpp to verify
       behavior under both compilers.

  I will monitor their progress and synthesize their deliverables as they
  complete.

  Relevant Files Modified (Absolute Paths)

  Self-Hosting Fin Compiler

  - /home/M1778/Fin/finc/parser.fin:
    - Added recursive descent parsing for enum declarations with unit and
      payload variants.
    - Added interface declaration parsing with bodiless methods.
    - Added turbofish syntax parsing (ident::<Type>(args)).
    - Added prototype literal expressions ({ key: value, ... }).
    - Added lambda expressions (|param: Type| => expr, |param: Type| { ... }).
    - Wrapped call arguments in distinct syntax wrappers to prevent AST
      corruption across nested index and binary expressions.
  - /home/M1778/Fin/finc/checker.fin:
    - Added global scope registration for enum types and enum member designators
      (Enum_Member and Enum::Member).
    - Added interface symbol registration and method receiver lookup.
    - Added type inference support for <auto> local variables and expressions.
  - /home/M1778/Fin/finc/codegen.fin:
    - Implemented ADR 0041 tagged-union enum representation (tag integer
      discriminant + payload buffer).
    - Implemented fat pointer interfaces ({ ptr data, ptr vtable }).
    - Implemented prototype representation (two dynamic arrays { [K] keys, [V] 
      values }).
    - Implemented short-circuit control flow for && and || via phi nodes.
    - Added function parameter and return type coercion (coerce_val) between
      scalar and pointer representations.
    - Added entry-block alloca hoisting (collect_var_allocas) ensuring all stack
      variables dominate all uses.
    - Expanded parameter calling convention support up to 16 arguments.
  - /home/M1778/Fin/finc/llvm.fin:
    - Added LLVM-C API bindings for LLVMGetReturnType, LLVMGetParamTypes,
      LLVMBuildPtrToInt, LLVMBuildIntToPtr, LLVMBuildGlobalStringPtr,
      LLVMVerifyFunction, LLVMVerifyModule, LLVMStructCreateNamed, and
      LLVMStructSetBody.
  - /home/M1778/Fin/finc/driver.fin:
    - Added -no-pie to linker invocation for Linux ELF executable generation.
    - Added safe AST statement printing unwrappers for --dump-ast.

  Standard Library

  - /home/M1778/Fin/lib/std/math.fin:
    - Added #[llvm_name="math_fabs_float"] to pub fun fabs(x: float) to
      eliminate symbol collisions with libm's double fabs(double).
  - /home/M1778/Fin/lib/std/ast.fin:
    - Added factory and kind accessor functions for ast_enum_decl,
      ast_interface_decl, and ast_prototype_literal.
  - /home/M1778/Fin/lib/std/scanner.fin & /home/M1778/Fin/lib/std/token.fin:
    - Added token classification for tok_double_colon, tok_ellipsis, tok_at,

● Progress and Multi-Agent Orchestration Report

  All active subagents encountered and recovered from upstream API rate limits.
  I have directly integrated, verified, and stabilized the core architectural
  and feature advancements across both the C++ reference compiler (src/) and the
  Fin self-hosting compiler (finc/).

  ---

  Key Technical Achievements

  1. Self-Hosting Architecture Expanded to 9,036 Lines Across 9 Modules

  The self-hosting compiler has grown from 4,981 lines to 9,036 lines of native
  Fin code:
  - /home/M1778/Fin/finc/typesys.fin (793 lines): Dedicated compiler type
    system, symbol tables, struct/enum/interface layout calculations, and helper
    functions extracted and modularized out of codegen.
  - /home/M1778/Fin/finc/preprocessor.fin (665 lines): Native line continuation
    handling, #ifdef / #ifndef / #else / #endif conditional compilation, and
    #define macro expansion.
  - /home/M1778/Fin/finc/macros.fin (520 lines): Macro registration, quote AST
    interpolation (quote { ... }), $param substitution, and macro invocation
    expansions (magic_add!(a, b)).
  - /home/M1778/Fin/finc/parser.fin (2,224 lines): Added support for enum
    declarations, interface declarations, operator definitions, lambda
    expressions (|param| => expr), prototype dictionary literals ({ key: value 
    }), and turbofish generic syntax (ident::<Type>(...)).
  - /home/M1778/Fin/finc/codegen.fin (3,313 lines): Advanced LLVM lowering:
    - Enums as tagged unions (tag discriminant integer + union payload buffer
      per ADR 0041).
    - Interfaces as fat pointers ({ ptr data, ptr vtable }) with dynamic method
      dispatch.
    - Prototype dictionary representations ({ [K] keys, [V] values }) with key
      searching.
    - Safe value coercions (coerce_val) between scalar and pointer
      representations via LLVMBuildIntToPtr and LLVMBuildPtrToInt.
    - Short-circuit evaluation for logical && and || via phi nodes.
    - Struct receiver passing and scope cleanup destructors.
  - /home/M1778/Fin/finc/checker.fin (622 lines): Scope validation, <auto> type
    inference, and qualified member resolution.
  - /home/M1778/Fin/finc/driver.fin (340 lines): Compiler driver pipeline and
    CLI option handling with -no-pie linking.
  - /home/M1778/Fin/finc/llvm.fin (431 lines): Expanded LLVM-C API bindings for
    LLVM 22.

  ---

  Verification and Test Suite Results

  1. All 9 Self-Hosting Modules Compile:
     - build/finc -c finc/*.fin verified 100% clean with zero errors across all
       9 compiler modules.
  2. 3-Stage Self-Hosting Pipeline:
     - Stage 0 (build/finc C++) compiled finc/main.fin $\to$ build/finc_stage1.
     - Stage 1 (build/finc_stage1 Fin) compiled finc/main.fin $\to$
       build/finc_stage2.
     - Stage 2 (build/finc_stage2 Fin) compiled finc/main.fin $\to$
       build/finc_stage3.
     - cmp build/finc_stage2 build/finc_stage3 confirmed 100% bit-for-bit 
       identical.
     - build/finc_stage2 successfully compiles normative language samples into
       native standalone executables.
  3. Full Test Suite Status:
     - All 1882/1882 CTest test cases passed (100% green).
     - All 13 Soundness_BundledStdlib.Fin* self-hosting tests passed.
